# Dataset Metadata
Dataset Name: abalone
Target Variable: predict the age of abalone from physical measurements
Sample Count: 1
Feature Count: 8
Label Distribution: younger(1)

# Feature Details
Feature 1: shucked_weight
- Type: float
- Description: weight of meat (grams)
- Stats: Min=0.7070, Max=0.7070, Mean=0.7070

Feature 2: diameter
- Type: float
- Description: perpendicular to length (mm)
- Stats: Min=0.4500, Max=0.4500, Mean=0.4500

Feature 3: length
- Type: float
- Description: longest shell measurement (mm)
- Stats: Min=0.6150, Max=0.6150, Mean=0.6150

Feature 4: viscera_weight
- Type: float
- Description: gut weight (after bleeding) (grams)
- Stats: Min=0.2095, Max=0.2095, Mean=0.2095

Feature 5: shell_weight
- Type: float
- Description: after being dried (grams)
- Stats: Min=0.2505, Max=0.2505, Mean=0.2505

Feature 6: height
- Type: float
- Description: with meat in shell (mm)
- Stats: Min=0.1500, Max=0.1500, Mean=0.1500

Feature 7: sex
- Type: categorical (Categorical)
- Description: sex of abalone
- Categories: male(male), female(female), infant(infant)

Feature 8: whole_weight
- Type: float
- Description: whole abalone (grams)
- Stats: Min=1.1980, Max=1.1980, Mean=1.1980

# Label Definitions
younger (Value=0): the age of abalone is less than or equal to 9
older (Value=1): the age of abalone is greater than 9

# Data Examples
样本 1: shucked_weight=0.7070, diameter=0.4500, length=0.6150, viscera_weight=0.2095, shell_weight=0.2505, height=0.1500, sex=male, whole_weight=1.1980 → younger
